- **research-market**: The market axis of the deep pass — mine ONE part of the market's structure deep from primary sources: how money moves in that segment, how people buy, and the observable sizes and spend, verbatim with URLs and dates. TRIGGER when one discovered part of the market structure needs its records extracted. DO NOT TRIGGER to enumerate the market's structure broad (discover-market), to mine one group's language (research-audience), or to study a competitor's own pages (research-competitor). - **research-method**: Applies a disciplined research method to conceptual, definitional, and "difference between X and Y" questions — especially overloaded terms and LLM/agent-domain topics, where answering from memory is a known trap. - **research-note-template**: **WORKFLOW SKILL** — Extract structured research notes from a source URL or document into a standardized YAML-frontmatter markdown note. Applies fixed extraction rules for consistent note-taking across all research areas.
